- [x] **Boss waves are gates (owner: "After wave 20 the boss got all the way to the vault but it still did not take me back like the old version. It just let me go on to wave 21. They should be a gate I can't get past.").**
  - Today `src/core/defense.js` drops back only when the vault's strength reaches 0, so a boss that leaks still counts as cleared. Fix: a boss wave is beaten only when its boss is killed. If the boss reaches the vault (or an outpost's stockpile), the boss wave is lost: the town drops back `breachBack` waves and loses the same coin share as a fallen vault, the vault refills, and a plain card says what happened and what to do ("The war wagon got through. Back to wave 18. Build more towers and try again.").
  - Every town and outpost, the bandit wagon in Dry Creek and Pine Hollow too. The next wave number never passes an unbeaten boss. Decide what a won outpost's raids do (they bring no boss) and write it in GDD.md.
  - Tests: a leaked boss means no wave 21; a killed boss passes. Rerun the bots against the pacing targets and retune if they miss. Add the rule to GDD.md.
  - In the town in play a lost boss also holds the waves: the player decides when to try again with Call now (owner, 2026-11-17).
  - Notes: shipped 2026.11.18-3. `waveEnd` now loses any boss wave whose boss got through, in play too (it was only away), so `fall(…, 'boss')` drops the town back 2 waves with the coin share and refills the vault; a won outpost's raids bring no boss, so they have no gate (decision, in GDD.md). A lost boss holds the waves in play as well (`town.lost.away` false; the breach event carries `away`), and the town in play shows a card (`lostCard` in `src/ui/lost.js`): "Wave 20 lost", "The war wagon reached your vault. You dropped back to wave 18 and lost 3.68K coins." and "Your waves are on hold. Build more towers, then tap Resume waves." with Got it. While held the wave bar says "on hold" instead of a countdown (the screenshot-only review caught "in 13s" next to "on hold"; it also reworded "got through" and all three What's new lines). Balance (the old numbers had let every boss leak): every bot lost the war wagon at wave 10 and then the wave-20 one again and again with half its health left. Fixes, all proved by `tools/balance.js`: a boss's health grows slower than its wave (`WAVE.bossGrowth` 0.4, `bossHp` in `defense.js`), war wagon 12 (was 20), bandit wagon 8 (was 10), armored train 21 (was 26; 23 lost seed 6's first train, 20 made seed 2 reach the Ridge's wave 10 in 6.9 min). The bots now play bosses like a careful player: towers a level higher while a boss wave is next (`BOSS.prep`), and after a loss a level higher and one tower more until it is down (`BOSS.retry`), towers before buildings and before goal savings meanwhile (the Ridge bot had saved for its goal building every time the vault refilled and lost the wave-20 train four times in a row), then Resume waves after `RETRY` 20 s (the newcomer 45 s). minmax keeps up to 7 towers (was 6) with at most one Cannon (two high Cannons did 38-41% of its damage) and in Dry Creek, where only the Crossbow exists, is not held to two; economy keeps its towers 0.3 of a level higher (its wave 10 was 27% behind defense). Results (6 seeds): Dry Creek won 9.3 min (active), Gulch wave 10 at 7.6 min, wave 20 at 16.4 min (was 16.8), first charter in range, Ridge wave 10 at 7.0-8.9 min, every strategy within 25%, variety ok; newcomer Dry Creek 12.2-12.9 min and Gulch wave 10 10.0-10.3 min. Also fixed, found by smoke on this build: a plot at the top edge of a landscape screen behind a tower's tap post opened the tower, because the ray past the post missed the plot and its ground; now the nearest other pad or plot gets the nearer-middle test (`pickTarget`). Tests: `test/bossgate.test.js` (5: a leaked boss means no wave 11 and a held town, Resume starts wave 8, a killed boss passes, Call now retries, Dry Creek is not won by a leaked bandit wagon, the card's words); the outpost test's towers are stronger so they kill the bandit wagon. Smoke checks the card, the drop back, the hold and Got it at every size; shots has a boss-lost frame. Checks: 227 tests, audit ok, smoke all ok, perf Gulch 18.3 ms and the empire gate 19.5 ms, update and install tests ok. Not yet tried on a real phone.
- [x] **Music and sound (moved up from M7 Juice and extras).**
  - Adapt `../tycoon/src/music.js` into `src/audio/`: procedural Web Audio, no files and no other hosts. An Old West feel: a calm theme while building and a tenser one during waves and bosses.
  - Basic sound effects: build, shot, coin and boss.
  - Music and Sound sliders in the Menu sheet (Settings), kept under a `cg-*` key. Sound starts only after the first tap (browser rules) and stops when the app is hidden.
  - No explainer needed; note it in What's new.
  - Notes: shipped 2026.11.18-4. `src/audio/tunes.js` is the old game's tune writer made a pure module (seeded mulberry32, so `test/audio.test.js` plays it without a sound card), and `src/audio/audio.js` is the player: one AudioContext made on the first pointerdown, keydown or touchend, a music bus with the canyon echo on the lead, and an effects bus. Four moods (decision: the owner asked for a calm build theme and a tenser wave and boss one, so the old town tune is the build theme at 96 bpm, and a new galloping wave tune at 118 bpm sits between it and the 132 bpm boss tune): title on the title screen, town between waves and on the empire map, wave while raiders are on the road, boss for a whole boss wave; a new mood starts on the next bar. Effects: a hammer knock for each building, tower and upgrade, a shot per tower kind (crossbow, cannon, frost, still, mortar, and the mortar shell landing), a coin blip per bounty, a low horn when a boss wave starts and a thud when a raider gets through. Only the town in play sounds, never during catch-up, each effect has a minimum gap and at most 14 play in a second. The Menu has a Volume section with Music and Sound sliders (5% steps, 0 reads Off; defaults 50% and 60%), kept under `cg-audio`; moving Sound plays a coin. Hidden or pagehide suspends the context, so nothing plays in the background, and a tap while hidden does not wake it. Tests: 7 in `test/audio.test.js` (the same bars for a seed, pitches in range, faster wave and boss tunes, the mood rule, cg-audio kept and a damaged entry ignored, no Web Audio breaks nothing, a stand-in context schedules bars, switches mood and goes quiet when hidden or Music is Off, every effect plays and a burst is thinned, effects only for the town in play and never in catch-up). Smoke at every size: a tap starts the music, the sliders sit under Volume and fit, both are kept and Off stops the bars, hidden suspends it and showing wakes it. `tools/perf.js` now starts the audio like a tap (`--no-audio` leaves it off) and reports it: the empire gate gave 18.9 and 19.4 ms with audio and 19.5 and 18.9 ms without, so the music costs nothing measurable; one earlier empire run hit 20.1 ms over its 20 ms limit, which is noise like the earlier runs (19.2-19.7 ms), but it is written here. New `tools/sfx-check.js` plays a Gulch wave headless and counts effects (19 shots and 6 kills gave 25 effects in 30 s). The screenshot-only review renamed the heading from Sound to Volume (it repeated the slider's name), asked for a bigger slider thumb (now 26 by 32 px on a 40 px tall slider), moved the What's new line off the title ("In your town, tap Menu…") and changed "for your phone" to "for this device" under Graphics. Left: it saw the slot line's coins differ from the top bar, which is the screenshot tool's debug coins, not a bug. No simulation change, so the bots were not rerun. Checks: 234 tests, audit ok, smoke all ok, perf Gulch 19.6 ms and the empire gate 18.9-19.4 ms (96 and 109 draw calls), update and install tests ok. Needs a listen on a real phone (iOS starts sound only after a tap, and the mix and volumes are untested on a phone speaker).
The Capital is split into three boxes so each ships on its own (decision 2026-11-18, as the Town layer was).
- [x] **The Capital opens, with federal contracts.** Founding it from Iron Ridge, its place on the map, federal contracts that pay coins and Influence, the bots building it at 2.5-4 h.
  - Notes: shipped 2026.11.18-5. `src/core/capital.js` (a system with only a tick, plus `claim`, which `sellSurplus` calls after routes and before the market) and `src/data/capital.js`. Decisions, all in GDD.md (The Capital): the Capital is not a town (no board, road or raiders; state in `game.shared.capital`, save v14 with a migration), so its card on the empire map is its screen and the player never flies in; it opens at Iron Ridge's best wave 30 for 150K coins from the purse and shows from wave 25 (the GDD's first sketch, wave 15 + 2M, was written before the economy was tuned; the bots hold 20-60K coins a minute in the Ridge's second hour); the treasury and its 10% tithe are dropped since there is one purse; one contract at a time, so the choice is which one (Take, or Switch with two taps, which drops the one running; Drop with two taps). An offer asks for a made good of an open town that has some to spare (what its buildings make, less what its own workshops and the towns it ships to use), about 4-8 minutes of that, rounded to two figures, no good twice, finished goods twice as likely; it pays 1.5 times base price in coins (into the purse as the maker's own earnings, "Contracts for the Capital" in the income explained) and 4 or 8 Influence for 6 minutes' worth by tier. The goods go like a route: above the workshops' buffer and the tower reserve, and the Sell switch does not stop them. On screen: the map's Capital badge shows Needs wave 30 in Iron Ridge, then Ready to build, then the running contract's good and percent and the Influence held, with a dot while nothing runs; Map carries a dot when it can be built or nothing runs (`mapCalls`); the card lists the rules with have / need and Build the Capital, then Influence, Working on (sent of need, a bar, about how long) and the offers; a toast when a contract is delivered; Places (no 3D) gets a Capital entry with the same body as a sheet. A first capitol model (white hall, columns, a copper dome, a flag) replaces the surveyed plot; the city is the third box. Explainers: "The Capital" on Map when it comes in sight, "Contracts" on Map once it is built (`capital:sight`, `contract`, `system:capital` in the audit). Bots: `tools/balance.js` `empire(..., { capital })` for the active seeds plays on in the Ridge, saves once the price is no more than 6 minutes of every town's income, holding the Ridge's waves and switching off every town's foremen (with them on, the other towns' foremen spent the purse and the Capital came at 4.2 h), then works the offer with the most Influence for 30 minutes; `capitalCheck` gates 150-240 min. Results (6 seeds): built at 3.09-3.42 h with 16-19 min of held waves, Ridge best wave 42-47, 5-6 contracts and 30-43 Influence in 30 minutes. Worth knowing for the Automation box: with one purse, a foreman anywhere spends what you are saving for; the spend share per town will matter. The screenshot-only review reworded the help cards, What's new, the waiting line ("Waiting. Iron Ridge has no spare pig iron. Make more pig iron there."), the empty-board line and the toast ("Pig iron delivered. You got 25.4K coins and 5 Influence."), dropped a footer that explained Switch, said where Influence goes ("Save your Influence. It will buy upgrades for all your towns in a later update."), and changed locked badges to "Needs wave 30 in Iron Ridge" and "Best wave 10". Left for Evaluation 3: toasts can sit over a card's title or a map badge for a few seconds (earlier reviews saw the same). Tests: `test/capital.test.js` (7: in sight, ready and built once from the purse; offers are spare made goods sized and paid as stated; a taken contract fills before the market even with Selling off, pays coins as the maker's earnings and Influence, and the board refills; the tower reserve is kept and a good with nothing spare is never asked for; Switch and Drop; the save keeps it and draws the same next offers; a v13 save loads). Checks: 241 tests, audit ok, balance ok (Dry Creek won 8.2-9.5 min, Gulch wave 10 7.1-8.0 min, charter offered 40.1-44.3 min, Ridge founded 66.0-71.2 min, Ridge wave 10 7.0-8.9 min, Boiler Shop 95-98%, strategies within 25%, variety ok), firstplay ok (newcomer 12.5 and 10.1 min), smoke all ok, perf Gulch 18.4 ms, the empire gate 19.8 ms (96 and 109 draw calls) and the map 17.1 ms, update and install tests ok. Not yet tried on a real phone.
- [x] **Turning the phone fills the screen (owner bug, 2026-11-18: "going from portrait to landscape left the right side blank").** Jumps the queue.
  - Notes: shipped 2026.11.18-6. Cause: `resize()` in `src/render/scene.js` set `canvas.style.width/height` to the first size in px, so `canvas.clientWidth` never changed again, and the town view, the empire map and the title all size themselves from it. Fix (left by the owner's main session, kept): the canvas stays at 100% from the CSS and only the drawing buffer is set in px. Checked the rest: `pick.js`, `camera.js` and `empire-view.js` read `getBoundingClientRect` or `clientWidth` at the moment they need it, and nothing else writes a pixel size to the canvas. New smoke section `rotate`: at 400x800, then 900x400, then back, on the title, in Copper Gulch and on the empire map, the canvas box must match the screen, the drawing buffer must have the screen's shape, no HUD box may be cut off, and in the town every pad and plot must open with a tap (`tapEverything`, three camera views) after each turn. Against the old scene.js it fails exactly like the owner's phone (the canvas stays 400x800 on a 900x400 screen, and taps on the right side land on the bare page); with the fix it passes. Also `tools/cdp.js` now removes its exit hook when a browser closes (the extra smoke browser tripped Node's 10-listener warning). Release checks: 241 tests, audit ok, balance ok (Dry Creek won 8.8-9.6 min, Gulch wave 10 7.1-8.9 min across strategies, charter offered about 40 min, variety ok), firstplay ok (newcomer Dry Creek 12.5 min, Gulch wave 10 10.1 min, never stuck over 1.2 min), smoke all ok with rotate, perf Gulch 19.1 ms median (96 draw calls, 58k triangles, 0.54 px raider step). Perf empire missed once at 20.2 ms on its 20 ms gate; an interleaved run of the last release and this one gave 19.8/19.5 and 19.8/19.3 ms, the same, so it is the machine, and this build then passed twice in a row. The empire gate has under 5% headroom on this machine; the next box that draws more must find some. Final build: Gulch 19.0 ms, empire 19.6 ms, both ok. Fresh-eyes text review (62 phone screens) fixed here: the locked Capital badge on the map was cut off ("Needs wave 30 in Iron Ridg"; the badge is wider and reads "Wave 30 in Iron Ridge"); the goal strip's Done flash showed the old "Wait for 4 more stone" words (it now shows the goal itself); "Save your Influence. It will buy upgrades ... in a later update" became "Keep your Influence. Soon it will buy upgrades for every town."; "got through in Copper Gulch. Its waves are on hold" became "broke into Copper Gulch. Its waves wait"; "saved for this upgrade" and "saved for towers" became "kept for this upgrade, not sold" and "kept for towers"; the damage source "Waves you beat before" is "Bonus on waves you beat before". Left for later: toasts sit over the Hold waves button and map labels for a few seconds (they let taps through), and the Perks screen's deed lines are dense.
- [x] **Institutions and the Empire tree.** Institutions founded once each with coins from the purse (Market Board, Department of Transportation, Bank, University, War Office; the Stock Exchange waits for M6). Each has its own branch of nodes bought with Influence, every effect a named source with a cap. Influence on screen wherever it can be spent. Explainers, tests, bots (a node every 10-15 minutes for the active bot), What's new.
  - Notes: shipped 2026.11.18-7. `src/data/institutions.js` and `src/core/tree.js` (a system: setup, init and load put the tree on every town; save v15 with a migration, `game.shared.tree = { founded, nodes, spent }`). Decisions, all in GDD.md (Institutions and the Empire tree): five institutions, each founded once from the purse at 50K for the first and 1.5 times more for each one after, whichever it is, so the order is the player's choice (first tried 100K doubling: the active bot built its first one 73-85 min after the Capital and the Influence piled up; then 50K and 1.8 times: the third came at 84-105 min). Each is a line of 5 upgrades at 8, 15, 25, 40 and 60 Influence (first tried 10, 20, 35, 55, 80: 24-30 min an upgrade). Each upgrade's effect is a named source on every town's stats under the institution's name (one row "Bank" in the explain view), inside the published caps; new stats `cost:build`, `cost:research` (family `cost`) and `influence` (cap 10, empire-wide on `game.mods`, which is rebuilt from the tree and not saved; a contract pays its Influence times it). Effects: Market Board prices and +25% Influence twice (the only way to earn Influence faster), Transport outposts and building costs, Bank coins earned and every cost, University research and range, War Office damage, vault, bounty and fire rate; University and War Office will open Auto-research and Auto-towers in the Automation box. The tree is empire-wide: a charter keeps it and a town opened later gets it. On screen: the Capital's card has two tabs, Contracts and Institutions; each row shows what it does, its cost as have / need (red when short) and Build or Buy, then "Bank · 2 of 5 upgrades" with the next upgrade; a toast for each; Influence stays at the top of the card and on the map badge; the Capital badge and Map get a dot when something in the tree can be bought (`treeCalls`). Explainer "Institutions" on Map once the first one can be built (`system:tree`, `institution` in the audit; the audit also checks the names are unique and every effect's stat has a cap). Bots: `tools/balance.js` plays 120 min after the Capital (was 30), founds in the order Market Board, Bank, War Office, University, Transport once the price is no more than 6 minutes of every town's income (saving for it like the Capital), buys the cheapest upgrade it can at once, and `capitalCheck` gates 10-15 min an upgrade: 10.9-13.3 min on 6 seeds (9-11 upgrades, 3-5 institutions, 134-212 Influence in 2 h). Market Board and Bank are always first for this bot by its order; Transport came last and was built on only 2 seeds, worth a look when Automation adds reasons to pick the University and War Office. The screenshot-only review renamed Found to Build (it read as "found it"), dropped the upgrade's name from the row (two names on one line, it stays in the toast), wrote "2 of 5 upgrades" and reworded the Capital's intro ("Your towns send goods here for coins and Influence. Spend Influence on upgrades for every town."). Found on the way: the pressed tab had gold text on a gold button. Left for Evaluation 3 (older lines, as earlier reviews also saw): toasts over map labels and card titles, the Pine Hollow badge under the coins on a phone, "Pig iron 96%" on the Capital badge, Switch not saying the goods sent are lost, the back card's wording, "kept for this upgrade, not sold", costs on pad and research cards without have / need, and the shots tool's Ridge "Best wave 0" (its debug state). Tests: `test/tree.test.js` (7: founding and the growing price; upgrades in order with Influence and the top; named sources on every town, outposts only where it says, inside the caps; the Market Board's Influence on a finished contract; a charter keeps it and a new town gets it; the save and the v14 migration; names and text). Smoke at every size: the tab lists 5 institutions with have / need, Build pays from the purse with its toast, Buy puts the Bank on income and reads 1 of 5 upgrades. Shots add capital-institutions. Checks: 248 tests, audit ok, balance ok (Dry Creek won 8.2-9.5 min, Gulch wave 10 7.1-8.0 min, charter offered 40.1-44.3 min, Capital 3.09-3.42 h, strategies within 25%, variety ok), firstplay ok (newcomer Dry Creek 12.5 min, Gulch wave 10 10.1 min), smoke all ok, perf Gulch 17.8 ms and the empire gate 18.7 ms (96 and 109 draw calls), update and install tests ok.   - Notes: shipped 2026.11.18-8. Decision: the city lives on the empire map (the Capital has no screen of its own, its card is its screen), drawn on a round stone plaza of radius 2.5 in place of the old lone capitol (`src/assets/models/capital.js`; layout `CITY` in `src/data/capital.js`). The capitol sits toward the back with its steps toward the camera's home side, the contract board in front of the steps (a white sheet pinned for each offer, a cream sheet with a red seal while a contract runs), and five lots round it: the Market Board (an open trading hall with a price board) at the plaza's front gate, the Bank (columns and a gold coin) and Transport (a brick depot with rails and a little engine) at the front corners, the University (brick hall with a tall clock tower) and the War Office (adobe fort, flag, cannon) at the back corners. Decision after the first look: with the University behind the capitol (the first layout) only its roof showed, so the capitol moved back and every building faces the camera. A lot shows survey stakes until its institution is built; then its building rises out of the lot in 0.9 s with a small overshoot (checked every frame, so it starts at once) and is merged back into the city, so the whole city is one model of 3 draw calls, rebuilt only when an institution is built or the board changes. The Capital's badge floats above the capitol's flag (it covered the dome). `siteRadius('capital')` is the plaza (was 2.2). Tests: `test/city.test.js` (3: a lot and a model for every institution; the capitol, the board, every lot, lamp and tree on the plaza and apart from each other; the plaza clear of every town). Smoke (map, every size): after the Bank is built it stands on its lot, nothing is still rising, the badge is above the city, and every road between two towns passes at least 0.45 outside the plaza. `tools/perf.js --town map` now builds the Capital and all five institutions. Shots add capital-city and capital-rise. The screenshot-only review reworded the What's new line (Capital and capitol in one line was confusing; now "a big domed hall") and the Department of Transportation's line ("More goods from outposts and cheaper buildings.", was "Busier outposts"), and the city shot now fits the whole plaza on a phone. Left for Evaluation 3, as earlier reviews also saw: toasts over a card's title or a map badge, the Capital badge's "Rails 40%" not saying what is 40% done, Influence not explained on screen beyond the Capital's intro, and a world-anchored badge going off the top edge when the camera is close. No simulation change, so the bots were not rerun. Checks: 251 tests, audit ok, smoke all ok, perf Gulch 18.0 ms (96 draw calls), the empire gate 18.1 ms (109) and the map with the full city 19.0 ms (41 draw calls, 113k triangles), update and install tests ok. Automation is split into three boxes so each ships on its own (decision 2026-11-18, as the Capital was). Background defense and offline catch-up shipped with "Towns keep playing out of sight".
- [x] **The Automation sheet.** Per-town switches: Foremen (Auto-build), Auto-towers (opened by the War Office) and Auto-research (opened by the University).
  - Notes: shipped 2026.11.18-9. `src/core/automation.js` (a system after the tree) and `src/data/automation.js`. Decisions, in GDD.md (Automation): every switch starts Off and only takes over a choice made by hand. Auto-towers raises the cheapest raise of a standing tower once every 3 minutes per town (0.85 times as long for each War Office upgrade), never builds one, and a level 4 tower waits for the player to pick its path. Auto-research, when the lab is idle, starts the cheapest next level of an item researched at least once. Foremen: the sheet switches every hired foreman of the town on or off. Spending: instead of the first sketch's per-town spend share, each one buys only when the price is at most half the coins on hand, like a foreman, so half the one purse always stays (the idle box proves the cap). The switches are empire state (`game.shared.auto`, save v16 with a migration), so a charter keeps them; they work out of play and during catch-up (coarse catch-up buys at most one tower level per chunk). On screen: an Auto button in the side column once the War Office or the University stands, with a dot until the sheet is first opened and gold while Auto-towers or Auto-research is on; the sheet has a row per switch with what it does, a live line (the next raise, what it waits for, the research running) and Turn on / Turn off (grayed until it opens). Explainer "Automation" on the Auto button (`system:automation` in the audit). The screenshot-only review changed On / Off to Turn on / Turn off (it could not tell state from action), put the half-coins rule in one wording everywhere ("It never spends more than half your coins."), reworded the explainer, the rows and What's new (which now names what opens each one), grayed the Foremen switch before a foreman is hired, and changed "Build a tower first." beside a lit switch to "Waiting for a tower. Build one first." Left for Evaluation 3: in landscape a sheet covers the coins and the side buttons (all sheets). Found on the way: a tower built in a step has its goods reserve only from the next step, so the market can sell them once in between (harmless in play; the tests switch selling off). Smoke fixes found on the way (both also failed on the live 2026.11.18-8 build, so they came before this box): the tower reserve check set timber to 0 while the town ran, so the Lumber Camp sometimes made one before the card was read (it now pauses the town); and `tapEverything` (rotate, landscape, zoomed) failed about 1 run in 3 on a plot at the top edge, because it aimed while the camera was still easing after the turn; it now waits for the camera to hold still (17 runs in a row ok after). No game change for either. Tests: `test/automation.test.js` (7: opens with the institutions and starts Off, with the button and its dot; the cheapest raise once per wait, half the coins kept, never a path; War Office upgrades shorten the wait; Auto-research only repeats hand-picked items, cheapest first; the Foremen switch; works out of play and while away; the save, a charter and the v15 migration). Smoke (`auto`, every size): no button before the War Office, then the button with a dot, the sheet's three rows with only Auto-towers open, Turn on raises a tower and shows the next raise. Shots add auto. Bots: every switch is off for them, so pacing is unchanged; balance ok (Dry Creek won 8.8-9.6 min by strategy, Gulch wave 10 7.1-8.9 min, Capital 3.09-3.42 h, an upgrade every 10.9-13.3 min, variety ok), firstplay ok (newcomer 12.5 and 10.1 min). Checks: 258 tests, audit ok, smoke all ok, perf Gulch 18.9 ms, the empire gate 19.3 ms and the map 19.0 ms, update and install tests ok. Not yet tried on a real phone.
- [x] **Keep trying the boss.** A per-town switch on the Automation sheet, "Keep trying the boss" (off by default; owner, 2026-11-17). On, a town that loses its boss drops back and keeps playing, so Auto-towers can upgrade until it breaks through. At most one coin loss per boss attempt, and the town's map badge shows it is retrying. Write it in GDD.md (Automation).
  - Notes: shipped 2026.11.18-10. A third switch on the Auto sheet (`retry` in `src/core/automation.js`), opened with Auto-towers by the War Office, Off by default. Decision: a lost boss still holds the town and costs one coin loss as before; with the switch on, the town starts its waves again by itself once its towers are stronger than when it lost (`towerPower`: any tower a level higher, or a new one), then plays back up the 2 waves and meets the boss again. Trying again at once was rejected because the same towers would lose the same boss in a loop, a coin loss every 3 minutes; waiting for a raise makes each try one loss against better towers. A loss on a boss wave counts whether the boss got through or the vault fell on it; a vault falling on any other wave still holds as before, and the try ends when the boss is beaten or the town falls further back (a charter or a vault). States: `retryState` is wait (Auto-towers will raise one), stuck (Auto-towers is off, has no tower, or every tower waits for its path), or climb. The map badge reads Retry soon while it waits and Retrying while it climbs, with no red dot, and Map's dot and the card's warning come back when it is stuck ("Go there and raise one."). The loss card, toasts and Welcome back line say its waves "start again by themselves after a tower goes up a level". The screenshot-only review caught a vague "It", "a lost boss" (reads as a boss that got lost), the boss named two ways on two screens, and Retrying shown while it was only waiting; all reworded (the sheet's header now says "Automation never spends more than half your coins."). It also noted the Auto sheet covering Pause in landscape; that layout predates this box, and the sheet scrolls. A new explainer (`retry`) points at Auto after a lost boss while the switch is off. Save v17 adds `retry` and `boss` to every town's switches (Off). `test/retry.test.js`: Off holds even after a raise; On waits through 10 min with no raise, then climbs and loses once more (2 losses, 2 tries); with Auto-towers it breaks through in play and in 3 h of catch-up with at most one loss per try; a killed boss ends the try; the save round trip and migration. Smoke: the row, switching it on, and the sheet line after a lost war wagon, at all three sizes. Shots: `auto-retry` and `map-retry`.
- [x] **The idle bot.** `tools/idle.js`: a player who sets the switches once and leaves the game to Automation for 12 hours, with the Capital reached by the active bot first. It reports income by the hour, what each switch bought and the most any one purchase took from the purse.
  - Notes: shipped 2026.11.18-11. `tools/idle.js` runs `empire(seed, 'active', { capital: true })` (the Capital and 2 h of the Empire tree), then `leave`: every open switch on in every town (Auto-towers, Auto-research, Keep trying the boss), every hired foreman working, Resume waves in every held town except one that lost its boss (Keep trying the boss has it). After that nothing is tapped for 12 h, stepped like an app left open with the Ridge in play. Decision: stepped, not catch-up, so every switch works at its real pace (a 12 h run is about 50 s a seed). `meter` watches every coin that leaves the purse and files it by the system that spent it (Auto-towers and Auto-research by their events, foremen, raiders breaking in, anything else), with the share of the coins on hand it took; `trade` is income from sales and contracts only. The first run found a real bug: at Iron Ridge's wall the waves stalled for 10-40 minutes with raiders standing still, because the active bot's 11 Deep Freeze lenses refreshed the 0.8 s freeze before it ran out. Fix: a raider that thaws cannot be frozen again for 1.2 s (`FREEZE` in `src/data/towers.js`, `thawT` on a raider, saved with the battle). 2.4 s was tried first. It left the bots' Ridge too weak at its wall, and seed 5 bought an Empire tree upgrade only every 20 min. Retunes for it: the Ridge's called waves grow faster (`rushMore` 4, was 3.5; with 1.2 s seed 5 reached the Ridge's wave 10 in 6.8 min; 69 s between waves was tried and did nothing, since the bot calls early), and the careful bot starts saving for an institution once it costs 10 minutes of every town's income (`TREE_SAVE_MINUTES`, was 6). Idle results (6 seeds): Capital at 3.11-3.60 h; income in the first idle hour 2.27-3.10M and in the twelfth 2.07-6.00M; every purchase took at most 50% of the coins on hand (the cap); Auto-towers bought 297-392 tower levels for 16-34M, foremen 22-64 levels for 2.9-9.2M, Auto-research 19-20 levels where the University stood (3 seeds); the Ridge's vault fell 106-128 times (in play it never holds), and the Gulch fell once while away and stayed on hold for the rest of the 12 hours on every seed; bosses beaten 5-18 times, 0-3 boss losses, 0-4 tries again. The target misses: see the fix box below. Tests: `test/freeze.test.js` (2: stacked lenses freeze a raider now and then, each freeze at most 0.8 s, frozen at most about 40% of the time and it keeps walking, and both fail on the old code; the wait survives a save) and `test/idle.test.js` (3: leave, the meter and its stop, the check). Checks: 270 tests, audit ok, balance ok (Dry Creek won 8.2-9.5 min, Gulch wave 10 7.1-8.0 min, charter offered 38.3-44.2 min, back in 38-55% of the first climb, Ridge founded 64.5-70.5 min, Ridge wave 10 7.1-8.9 min, strategy medians 7.5-8.4 min, Capital 3.11-3.60 h, an upgrade every 10.9-15.0 min, variety ok), firstplay ok (newcomer Dry Creek 12.5 min, Gulch wave 10 10.1 min), smoke all ok (the first run failed one rotate tap, the Saw Mill at the top edge of a zoomed landscape view at y 9 px opening pad 7, the flake an earlier box saw; nothing here touches drawing or picking, and the rerun passed), perf Gulch 17.6 ms and the empire gate 17.9 ms (96 and 109 draw calls), update and install tests ok. The screenshot-only review reworded all three What's new lines (it read "for good" as "well", and asked to name Call now). It also saw, as before, that in landscape the What's new card covers the title's tagline over a barely dimmed menu, and that the version line means nothing to a child; left for Evaluation 3. Not yet tried on a real phone.
- [x] **Idle income grows (the idle target missed, 2026-11-18).** Over 12 idle hours the income from sales and contracts stayed flat to the cent on every seed (0.77-0.92M an hour), and total income went up and down with the Ridge's bounties.
  - Causes the bot showed: the only foremen the careful bot hires are on camps (the Gulch's Lumber Camp, the Ridge's Stone Quarry) whose goods already flood the market (timber and stone selling 1,400 a second), so a foreman spends millions on levels that earn nothing; Auto-towers spends 2 to 6 times what the foremen do; the Gulch falls once while away and then holds for the rest of the day; and the Ridge sits at its wall, breaking in about 10 times an hour.
  - Decide and fix, in this order: a foreman should wait on a level that would not pay back (a flooded good, no workshop short of it) and its card should say so in plain words; the careful bot should hire foremen along the chain, not only on camps; decide whether the gate is sales and contracts every hour (the economy Automation grows) with total income over 3-hour blocks, and write it here and in GDD.md (Automation). Rerun `tools/idle.js` and `tools/balance.js`.
  - Notes: shipped 2026.11.18-12. **Foremen wait** (`foremanWait` in `src/core/foremen.js`): while their building is short of an input, and while the next level would take over an hour (`PAYBACK`) to earn back its price, counting one more unit sold at base price times saturation squared (`marginal` in `src/core/market.js`, since selling more lowers the price of the rest). A level is always wanted when a workshop in the town, in a town it ships to, or (for an outpost) in any town is short of the good, or when the towers keep more of it than the yard holds. The card says why in plain words ("The foreman is waiting. Timber sells for very little now. No workshop needs more timber."). **The careful bot** hires foremen highest up the chain first (`hireOrder`), only where the next level is worth buying, and once the Capital stands it tends the towns it is not playing (`tendTowns`): one level a minute in each that pays back within 30 minutes, and a foreman. **The gate, decided:** the data showed sales sit near the market's ceiling (the sum of price times depth over every good, with the price bonuses, `ceiling` in `tools/idle.js`), 85-87% of it on every seed; the rest is bricks the Ridge uses up and wagons nobody makes. No foreman can raise that, so "sales rise every hour" cannot pass, and growing past it is what the next town is for. What an idle empire grows is its waves (Auto-towers, and bounties with them). 3-hour blocks were tried first and failed seed 6 on noise (11.2M, 9.4M, 9.8M, 14.7M: a town at its wall breaks in and replays waves, so bounties swing by 2M an hour). The gate is now: the second 6 idle hours earn more than the first 6 and the last 3 more than the first 3 (leaving out contracts, which stop once nobody takes them), sales every hour at least 95% of the first hour's, no purchase over half the coins, the Capital at 2.5-4 h. Results (6 seeds): halves 18.4-22.4M then 26.0-32.2M, first and last 3 hours 8.5-10.6M then 12.7-20.2M, sales 0.96-1.04M every hour, the Ridge's best wave 51-53 to 59-60, foremen 61-124 levels for 2.6-4.3M (before: millions on camps), Auto-towers 333-388 levels for 36-44M, every purchase at most 50% of the coins, Capital 3.08-3.25 h (was 3.11-3.60: no coins wasted on flooded camps). Retunes: each institution costs 1.6 times the last (was 1.5), because the fastest seed then bought an Empire tree upgrade every 9.2 min (now 10.0-12.0). The min-max test now checks its own rule (two of a kind and one Cannon, at most 7) instead of 6 towers, since the coins foremen saved bought it a seventh. Checks: 275 tests, audit ok, balance ok (Dry Creek won 8.8-9.6 min by strategy, Gulch wave 10 7.1-8.9 min, charter offered about 41 min, Ridge founded 64.6-69.0 min, Ridge wave 10 7.5-8.3 min, variety ok), firstplay ok (newcomer 12.5 min and 10.1 min), idle ok, smoke all ok (one run failed the known rotate flake, a tap at the top edge of a zoomed landscape view; the rerun passed), perf Gulch 18.0 ms and empire 19.4 ms (96 and 109 draw calls), update and install tests ok. The screenshot-only review rewrote the foreman lines (they lacked a subject), named the good on Keep for towers ("Keep 19 timber for towers"), and fixed a vague "it" in What's new and the Keep trying the boss line. It also saw, as before, map labels piling up under "The Capital is built." and a card covering the goods strip in landscape; left for Evaluation 3. Not yet tried on a real phone.
- [x] **Target (changed 2026-11-18, see the box above):** over 12 idle hours the second half and the last 3 hours earn more than the first, sales hold, spending never passes its cap, and the Capital opens at 2.5-4 h. Was: income rises every hour, which the market's ceiling rules out.
  - Notes: passes on 6 seeds with 2026.11.18-12 (`node tools/idle.js` prints ok). M4 is done.

### M5. Silver Falls, pacing, then Evaluation 2
Decision (2026-11-18): the Silver Falls box is split in two, like Iron Ridge's, so each ships on its own: the town in the game first, then the bots that pace it.
- [x] **Silver Falls and Sandy Bend in the game.** The power grid, the river and its boats, the steamer, models, and the 5-deed gate shown on the map. It opens Outpost 3, Sandy Bend (sand and quartz).
  - Notes: shipped 2026.11.18-13. **The river lane** (`src/core/defense.js`): `lanesOf(town)` gives the road (lane 0) and, where a board has `river`, the river (lane 1), both ending at the vault. A raider kind with `water` rides the river (`laneFor`); a river town sends every `waves.boatEvery`-th raider by boat (3 in the Falls), by position in the queue, not a random draw, so other towns' waves are untouched. Moving, leaking, splash, shells' lead and the background battle all read each raider's own lane. New raiders: the Riverboat (2.2 times a raider's health, armor 0.2, leak 2) and the ironclad steamer, the Falls' boss (20 times, armor 0.45, leak 10), a gate like every boss. Every tower hits boats (decision: the river's threat is a second lane to cover, not a tower that can't reach it); the Mortar's and the Still's "Best against" cover the steamer as a boss and armored. **The power grid** (`gridOf`, `onGrid`, `isGrid` in `src/core/economy.js`): power is a good in the data (`grid: true`) only so the chain rule and the cards can name it. The Water Wheel makes 1 a second per level, each workshop uses 1 for each unit it makes, it is never kept, sold, shipped, put on the strip or listed in Goods, and when the workshops want more than the grid makes they all run at the same share and show as short. Foremen see the grid (`shortOf`, `foremanWait`); the map's short-input line skips power unless the grid is short. **Data:** `src/data/towns/falls.js` (8 workshops, the road switching back four times, the river winding down the middle under three bridges to the same vault, 12 pads and 4 mortar pits clear of both lanes, the canyon builder for the road's rock cut in Falls granite, `bend` for the river), `src/data/towns/sandybend.js` (Sand Wash, Quartz Diggings, Crossbow and Cannon paid with sand, Pine Hollow's waves, `riverbed`), goods sand, quartz, power, glass, wire, light bulbs, dynamos, telegraphs (each step 1.40 times its inputs), seven research items with Live Wires as the trade-off, Falls tower costs in sand, quartz and glass. The Wire Mill makes 0.4 a second per level so the Gulch's Smelter keeps up at equal levels (a test checks every supplier across towns). **Founding** takes deeds as well (`found.deeds`, `deedsOf`, kept not spent): Ridge wave 25, 5 deeds, 500K coins (first pass; the GDD's 50M came before the purse). The map card shows "Hold 5 deeds. Sign a charter to get more." with have / need. **Screen:** water ribbon and green banks, plank bridges raised so boats go under and raiders climb over (`BRIDGE`, smoothed so nothing hops), both entrances with their plumes, boats bob on the water, the river on the map diorama; the Water Wheel card says what the workshops use and every workshop on a short grid says "Not enough power. Every workshop here runs slower. Upgrade the Water Wheel."; Power has no Sell button. Models in `src/assets/models/falls.js` (10 buildings), the boat and the steamer in `units.js`, `bend` and `riverbed` in `entrances.js`. Explainers: Silver Falls in sight (deeds), welcome (the river), Power, the steamer, Sandy Bend; the audit now requires `river` and `power` and checks pits and entrances on both lanes. Tests: `test/falls.test.js` (10). Tools: `shots.js falls`, `perf.js --town falls`, `?start=falls`. Checks: 285 tests, audit ok, balance ok, firstplay ok (newcomer Dry Creek 12.5 min, Gulch wave 10 10.1 min), idle ok (no simulation change before the Falls), perf Falls 22.1 ms median (104 draw calls, 65k triangles, raider step 0.58 px, max 1.17), Gulch 18.6 ms and the empire gate 19.6 ms, smoke all ok (its map check now expects the two new sites; one run missed a tap at the top edge of a landscape screen while the bots loaded the machine, and six runs of the tap check alone at that size, Gulch and Falls, missed none), update and install tests ok. The screenshot-only review asked the map's power line to say what to do (now "Upgrade the Water Wheel."), the deeds line to be shorter ("Hold 5 deeds. Sign a charter to get more."), and the Water Wheel's own card to say "Tap Upgrade."; done. Left as they are: the sighted card listing what the town will make while the open card lists what it makes now (the Ridge works the same way), "Each one uses" (every workshop card says it), toasts and cards covering the HUD for a moment, and the steamer's stack poking through a bridge as it passes under. Not yet tried on a real phone, and no bot plays the Falls yet (the next box).
- [x] **Bots for Silver Falls and Sandy Bend.** `tools/balance.js` plays on from the Ridge: signs charters until the empire holds 5 deeds, saves for the Falls (500K for now; tune it so the Falls opens at 6-10 h with charters and at least 2.5 times later without), wins Sandy Bend and plays the Falls with the Gulch sending copper and the Ridge steel and rails. Pacing: the Falls' wave 10 and the steamer (7-10 min after getting there, no breach in waves 1-3), the Wire Mill running on Gulch copper at least 90% of the time, the Water Wheel kept up (the grid's share at least 90% on average), Sandy Bend won within 20 min, every strategy within 25%, and every tower kind the best buy somewhere on two lanes. `tools/balance.js` counts a pad's reach over both lanes (its `near` reads only the road today). Give the Falls a goal strip through its chain (it has none yet). Write the numbers in GDD.md.
  - Notes: shipped 2026.11.18-14. **The run** (`tools/balance.js`, `empire(..., { capital: true, falls: true })`, `toFalls`): after the Capital's 2 hours the active bot plays on in the Ridge, signs the Ridge's charter once it pays the deeds the Falls still needs (`signAt` on `play`; it pays 6 at wave 48-50, so 7 held), climbs back, saves like the Capital (founding no institution meanwhile, `r.noInst`), founds the Falls, wins Sandy Bend, then plays the Falls for an hour with goals on, tending the Gulch and the Ridge for copper, steel and rails (`tendImports`) and filling contracts. Every strategy then plays the Falls for 30 minutes from the active player's arrival on seeds 1-3 (`fallsFrom`, a save of that moment), so they are compared on the same start. **Decisions:** the wait for the Falls is the Ridge's wave, not its coins: after the charter the Ridge is back past 50 in about 35 minutes and then climbs slowly, while the purse earns 30K-2M a minute (contracts make it jumpy), so any price the 6-minute saving rule reaches is no wait at all. So the Falls now takes Ridge wave 57 (was 25) and 1M coins (was 500K). Without a charter it never opens (5 deeds, the first charter pays 1), which is the "at least 2.5 times later" half of the target. The Falls' waves are 70 s apart (was 66) and a called wave grows with `rushMore` 6 (was 4): at 66 and 4 a called wave 10 came 6.8-7.0 min after getting there. A pad's reach (`cover`) counts both lanes, each weighted by the share of raiders on it (a third by boat). The bot raises the Water Wheel first when the grid is short or the next workshop level would make it short (`gridFirst`). **Goal strip:** the Sand Pit to level 4 (added after the screenshots showed an empty strip on arrival), Water Wheel, Glassworks, Water Wheel to 5, Wire Mill, a Smelter in the Gulch if there is none, Bulb Works, Dynamo Works, a Steel Mill and a Rolling Mill in the Ridge if its charter took them, Telegraph Works (within 600 min of a new game; the bot 384-424). **Results** (6 seeds): Ridge charter at wave 48-50 for 6 deeds, the Falls founded at 6.22-6.84 h of play (10-18 min held to save), Sandy Bend won in 9.7-10.7 min with no breach, the Falls' wave 10 (the steamer) at 7.7-8.2 min with no breach in waves 1-10, best wave 41-45 after an hour, the Wire Mill on Gulch copper 98-100% of the time, the grid at 100%, every workshop up within 8 minutes (the purse is large by then). By strategy (seeds 1-3, median wave 10): active 8.1, minmax 8.1, swarm 8.6, economy 7.8, defense 8.0 min, all within 25%. Every tower kind is built in the Falls (minmax builds no Cannon there), and the Cannon, Crossbow, Mortar and Frost Lens each lead some wave. **Seen, left for the next box:** the Mortar does 50-63% of the Falls' damage for every strategy (the Gulch's gate is 36%), and the Still at most 6%. Checks: 290 tests (`test/fallsbots.test.js`, 5 new), audit ok, balance ok (all earlier targets hold: Dry Creek 8.8-9.6 min by strategy, Gulch wave 10 7.1-8.0 min, Ridge founded 63.0-68.9 min, Capital 3.08-3.25 h), firstplay ok (newcomer 12.5 and 10.1 min), idle ok, smoke all ok, perf Gulch 20.6 ms and Falls 23.6 ms (104 draw calls, 65k triangles), the empire gate 19.7 ms on its passing run. The machine was slower this run (OneDrive syncing): the empire gate measured 20.3-21.3 ms on six other runs, and the published 2026.11.18-13 measured 21.3 ms in the same minute, so the build is no slower; the phone check with 4 boards in the next box looks again. The screenshot-only review rewrote two What's new lines ("the Goal box at the top tells you what to build next", "Tap Call now there and the wave brings more raiders"). Left as before for Evaluation 3: the "Silver Falls is open." toast over Hold waves, "Each one uses", "Vault", the map card's "3 / 5" wrapping, the town's two "makes" lines, and the "Tap Build to build" goal lines every town uses (the Build button is on the card the tap opens). `tools/shots.js` set the Falls' old price and failed to found it; fixed. Not yet tried on a real phone.
Decision (2026-11-18): "Pacing and Evaluation 2" is split like the earlier big boxes. The tower balance in the Falls, and the pacing it moved, ship first.
- [x] **No tower carries Silver Falls (split from Pacing and Evaluation 2).** The Mortar did 50-63% of the Falls' damage for every strategy (the Falls bots, 2026.11.18-14). Bring every kind under the 36% the Gulch gates on, add the Falls to `towerBalance`, and retune the pacing it moves.
  - Notes: built as 2026.11.18-15 and committed, **not yet published** (see Perf below): the next run reruns the release checklist and pushes it, with its tag and push notification, once the empire perf gate prints ok. **Why:** by the Falls the purse raises every tower to about level 13 (the bots raise to a level target, so coins stop mattering), and at the same level the 4 Mortars out-hit everything, with the east pit by the rock cut giving them every raider first (damage counts for the tower that lands it, up to the raider's health). Tried and dropped: moving that pit back alone (2 points), slower Mortar growth alone (the Cannon, then the Frost Lens took over at 37-42%), a Longbow trim (broke Dry Creek and the Gulch's min-max wave 20 through boss losses), faster poison (min-max's Still stood near the vault, where it never got a shot), and a bot that values road near the entrance more (min-max's Crossbow went to 46%). **Decisions:** a tower that hits a group grows slower each level than the Crossbow, which hits one raider (`grow` in `src/data/towers.js`: Cannon 1.25, Frost Lens 1.24, Mortar 1.19, Crossbow 1.3; the Still's poison keeps 1.15). The Falls has 3 mortar pits, not 4: the east pit is gone, the pit by the vault takes its number 13, and the north pit sits 1 farther back, at (2, -4.2). Save v18: a Mortar by the vault keeps its place; one on the east pit moves to the vault pit, or, if that pit had one too, comes down with every coin spent on it back in the purse. The min-max bot keeps at most two Crossbows and two Mortars and one of each other kind (was two of a kind and one Cannon): in the Falls its two lenses and two Crossbows took turns at 37-39% with its Still doing nothing; in the Gulch one lens alone left two Stills at 41%. **Pacing it moved, retuned:** the Ridge's wall got harder, so its charter pays the 5 deeds at wave 45-47 and the Falls now takes Ridge wave 52 (was 57; at 57 it opened at 8.7-9.4 h and never on seed 6); the armored train has 19 times a raider's health (was 21; the active bot lost it once on half the seeds); the steamer 18 (was 20; lost once on seed 1); each branch's first Empire tree upgrade costs 10 Influence (was 8; seed 2 bought one every 9.2 min again). **Results** (`tools/balance.js`, 6 seeds, all ok): Dry Creek won 8.2-9.5 min, Gulch wave 10 7.1-8.1 min, Ridge founded 63.2-70.2 min, Ridge wave 10 7.1-8.9 min, Capital 3.17-3.53 h, a tree upgrade every 10.0-13.3 min, the Ridge charter at wave 45-47 for 5 deeds, the Falls founded at 6.93-7.55 h, Sandy Bend won in 9.7-10.7 min, the Falls' wave 10 at 7.7-8.3 min with no breach. The most any kind does, by strategy: Gulch (still 28, cannon 29, mortar 31, cannon 28, still 33%), Falls (mortar 29, crossbow and frost 30, mortar 35, cannon 33, mortar 28%), every kind leads some Falls wave. Firstplay ok (newcomer 12.5 and 10.3 min), idle ok (Capital 3.17-3.53 h, first idle hour 1.95-3.08M, twelfth 2.21-3.35M, every buy at most 50% of the coins). Tests: `test/growth.test.js` (3: the growth order and level 1 unchanged; 3 pits, Mortar only, map rules; the v18 migration in all three cases), the Falls tests now expect wave 52. Smoke all ok (its Capital checks now wait up to 3 s for the map's once-a-second diorama sync, which a loaded machine missed, and read the first upgrade's price from the data). Perf: Gulch 20.4 ms, Falls 25.4 ms (104 draw calls, 65k triangles), both ok; the empire gate measured 20.6-21.8 ms against its 20 ms limit all evening, with other programs using about a core, and the published 2026.11.18-14 measured 20.8-21.2 ms in alternating runs, so the build is no slower (no drawing code changed), but the gate is red, so the push waits. Update and install tests ok. Published by the next run after the empire gate changed (see the M3 target's decision): empire 21.9 ms frame, 7.3 ms script, 109 draw calls, ok. The screenshot-only review rewrote four What's new lines (what "add less" means, what happened to a Mortar on the pit that went, what wave 52 is for, "institution"). Not yet tried on a real phone.
- [x] **Fresh-eyes fixes from 2026.11.18-15 (split from Pacing and Evaluation 2).** What the screenshot-only review of 2026.11.18-15 flagged.
  - Notes: shipped 2026.11.18-16. **Map badges** never cover each other (`liftBadges` in `src/ui/map.js`, `test/badges.test.js`): the badge lowest on screen, the nearest town, keeps its place and each one above it that would cover a placed badge is lifted just over it; sizes are measured with the 4-a-second paint. A badge never sits under the top bar's boxes (the place name and buttons on the left, the coins on the right): one whose town is under them sits just below, and one that lifting would push under them moves down below the badge it meets instead (the first try lifted Iron Ridge and Pine Hollow under the buttons in landscape). **The locked town card**: the deeds rule reads "Deeds 3 / 5" with "To earn deeds, tap Perks in a town and Sign the charter." under it while short (was "Hold 5 deeds. Sign a charter to get more." wrapping its count); an outpost's badge says "Start Iron Ridge" (was "Opens with Iron Ridge"). **What's new in landscape**: its list could not shrink below its lines (a grid row's minimum height), so the last lines and Got it fell below the screen; `.newsbody` has `min-height: 0` and `tools/smoke.js` (news) now checks at 860x400 that Got it is on screen and the last line can be scrolled to. **Words**: the Institutions tab reads "Bank · 1 of 5 bought" (was "1 of 5 upgrades"), the Department of Transportation (renamed from the British form of the name, which the audit now refuses) "Outposts make more goods. Buildings cost less.", the Capital's badge "Rails 40% sent", and a tower's Damage "36 a hit". Decision: shots.js builds its Silver Falls map without Pine Hollow, so its badge there reads "Start Iron Ridge" with the Ridge open; in the game founding the Ridge opens Pine Hollow too (`foundTown`), so only the screenshot shows it. Checks: 298 tests, audit ok, smoke all ok (one run missed a tap on a pad at the top of a turned landscape town view, the known rotate flake; untouched code, and the two runs after passed), perf Gulch 20.5 ms (6.9 ms script, 96 draw calls) and empire 20.7 ms (6.8 ms script, 109 draw calls), update and install ok, a screenshot-only review of the new screens (its remaining points are listed in the box below).
- [x] **Fresh-eyes fixes from 2026.11.18-16 (split from Pacing and Evaluation 2).** What the screenshot-only review of 2026.11.18-16 flagged.
  - Notes: shipped 2026.11.18-17. **Damage breakdowns add up as written** (`mulShown` in `src/ui/explain.js`): each factor is rounded to 1 or 2 decimals, then 2, then 3 if the written product would not read as the total; only at 3 decimals may a factor round the other way. `fmt` floors with 1e-9 of slack, so float math like 56.99999999999999 reads 57. **Sell price**: the game was right (a tower's `spent` counts every level); `tools/shots.js` set level 6 without its spend, so the screenshot sold for 60. The scene now adds up the real costs (the card reads 1.41K) and a test guards it. **Words**: the Capital's map label reads "Contract 40% done" (was "Locomotives 40% sent"); every build goal reads "Tap the Sawmill, then tap Build" (was "Tap Build to build the Sawmill"); a town's card says "Makes ..." in the same words before and after it opens (`makesWords`: its `makesText`, or every good it makes; the open card used to list only goods made so far); the deeds line reads "tap Perks in a town, then tap Sign the charter." **The waiting line** "You will have enough copper ore in about 4 seconds." is plain ink, not red (`.soon`), and under 20 seconds it drops "Upgrade the Copper Mine to make it faster." (the review called that advice silly for a 1-second wait). **Toasts** step aside from an open card (`clearOfCards` in `src/ui/play.js`): beside a side card in landscape, above a bottom card in portrait. Decision: building the Capital shows no toast at all, since its card turns into the Capital's own card (the toast moved above the card then sat on the Capital's map label). **Scenes made honest** in `tools/shots.js`: the armored train scene and the map keep the Ridge's best wave with its wave, and the Silver Falls map has Pine Hollow as founding the Ridge leaves it. Tests: `test/review16.test.js` (2: every tower kind, path and level 1-20 with a research bonus reads its total as written; selling a level 6 tower gives back a share of every level). Smoke: the tower card's waiting line names the Lumber Camp or says when, and is not red when it is good news; no toast covers or repeats the Capital card. Checks: 300 tests, audit ok, balance ok (unchanged, no simulation change: Dry Creek won 8.8-9.6 min by strategy, Gulch wave 10 7.2-8.9 min, Ridge founded 67.5-71.7 min, Falls founded 6.93-7.55 h, Falls wave 10 7.7-8.3 min), firstplay ok (newcomer 12.5 and 10.3 min), idle ok, smoke all ok (twice before the review fixes, once after), perf Gulch 19.4 ms (6.4 ms script, 96 draw calls) and empire 21.7 ms (7.1 ms script, 109 draw calls), update and install ok. Left for Evaluation 3, as before: in landscape a card covers the coins and the side buttons and cuts "Hold waves"; the cost row shows coins as a price, not have / need like the goods (the coins are always at the top); "Vault", "Institutions", "path" and "+26/s" with no unit; locked labels like "Wave 30 in Iron Ridge" read as fragments. Not yet tried on a real phone.
Decision (2026-11-19): "Pacing and Evaluation 2" is split again. The long runs, the phone check with every board and the fresh-eyes review ship first, with the stalls they found.
- [x] **Evaluation 2: the long runs and what they found (split from Pacing and Evaluation 2).** A 24 h idle run, a newcomer run past 2 h, phone performance with every board working, a fresh-eyes review of the screenshots and the bot tables, and the fixes.
  - Notes: shipped 2026.11.19-1. **What the long runs found.** The 24 h idle run passed its gate, but on every seed Copper Gulch sat on hold for the whole day at waves 27-33 while Auto-towers kept raising its towers: its vault fell on a plain wave while away, which holds the town, and Keep trying the boss only took up a lost boss. The newcomer run (`tools/firstplay.js` with 180 minutes) showed both of its towns on hold from about an hour in until the end: it held the Gulch's waves to save for Iron Ridge, as the goal strip says, and nothing ever asked it to start them again; then the Ridge's vault fell while the strip had sent it to the Gulch for a Toolsmith (still locked there, so that goal sat on the strip for two hours). **Fixes:** Keep trying the boss also takes up a vault that fell on a plain wave (`lostTry` in `src/core/automation.js`; its name stays the owner's, its Auto sheet line reads "After a boss gets through or the vault falls, waves start again when a tower goes up a level."); founding a town starts the waves the paying town held to save for it, with "The waves in Copper Gulch start again." on the toast (a hold from a loss stays); before any other goal the strip says "Tap Resume waves" while the town in play holds (not while it saves for its next town or can found it), then "Go to Iron Ridge and tap Resume waves" for a town that lost while away with Keep trying off (`RESUME`, `goResume` in `src/core/goals.js`; the careful bots read the strip with `goalNow(game, false)` and tap Resume by their own rules, so their numbers did not move). With the Gulch playing again, the idle halves gate missed by a hair on two seeds (9.00M then 8.83M; 19.08M then 19.00M): the Gulch climbs about 10 waves in the first hours, then waits for Auto-towers to afford its next raise, while Auto-towers' biggest buys (up to 393K) went to Pine Hollow and Dry Creek. Decision: in a won outpost Auto-towers raises a tower only after a raid got through, one raise per raid lost (`raidsHeld`; its line reads "Raises a tower when a raid gets through."; `auto.raids` is an optional field read as 0, so no save migration). New gate: `tools/firstplay.js` fails a town on hold over 10 minutes at a stretch while not saving for its next town (`HELD_LIMIT`); it also records the first trip to each town, since the Resume step sends it back later. **Results:** newcomer ok (Dry Creek 12.2-12.9 min, Gulch wave 10 10.0-10.3 min, Ridge founded 43.4-55.8 min, longest hold not saving 0.2-0.3 min, was 51-66 min; at 3 h the Gulch reaches waves 37-41 and the Ridge 33-36, were 21-26 and 30-34, both on hold). Idle 12 h ok on 6 seeds (Capital 3.17-3.53 h, the Gulch climbs from 29-37 to 38-47 where it sat still, the Ridge to 55-57, was 53-56, every buy at most 50% of the coins). Idle 24 h before the outpost change: ok, sales 0.96-1.02M every hour, the Ridge 47-49 to 56-58. Balance ok and unchanged (Dry Creek 8.2-9.6 min, Gulch wave 10 7.2-8.9 min, Ridge founded 63.2-70.2 min, Capital 3.17-3.53 h, Falls founded 6.93-7.55 h, Sandy Bend 9.7-10.7 min, Falls wave 10 7.7-8.3 min, every strategy within 25%, variety ok). **Phone with every board:** `tools/perf.js --town falls-empire` (new) plays Silver Falls with the Gulch, the Ridge, the Capital, Dry Creek, Pine Hollow and Sandy Bend working behind it: 24.3 ms median frame, 7.9 ms script, 104 draw calls, 65k triangles, raider step 0.99 px (max 1.19); Gulch 20.4 ms, the empire gate 21.4 ms (7.0 ms script, 109 draw calls). **Decided, kept:** the bots keep valuing a pad by the road it covers. Weighting road near the entrance was tried in 2026.11.18-15 and made min-max's Crossbow carry 46%, and the variety gate already reads damage from the real game, so a placement that leaves a tower idle shows there (min-max's Still in the Falls, 0%, is its own rule of one of each kind). **Fresh-eyes review** (screenshots and bot tables only): fixed the lost toast ("Go there and tap Resume waves", was "Its waves wait until you go there"), the map's retry lines ("Auto-towers will raise a tower. Then Copper Gulch fights the war wagon again.", and "wave 28" for a fallen vault), the back button's card ("Tap the ◂ button beside Map to go back to the town you played last."), the old-ground bonus label ("Waves you beat before", the card "Towers hit harder on old waves"), the Capital's line ("Takes goods from your towns for coins and Influence. Influence buys upgrades for every town."), and the charter line no longer repeats the deeds you hold. A second screenshot-only review before publishing: the goal strip now says "Build more towers, then tap Resume waves" after a loss, as the lost card does (`RESUME_LOST`, same tap for the bots); the lost toast names the buttons ("Tap Map, then Copper Gulch, then Resume waves."); the Auto line reads "After a boss gets through or the vault falls" (was "a lost boss or a fallen vault"). Its other points are in the box below. Checks (re-run by the run that published it, after the last fixes): 308 tests, audit ok, balance ok (Dry Creek 8.8-9.6 min by strategy, Gulch wave 10 7.2-8.9 min, every strategy within 25%, variety ok), firstplay ok (newcomer Dry Creek 12.5 min, Gulch wave 10 10.3 min, Ridge founded 43.4-55.8 min, longest hold not saving 0.2-0.3 min), idle 12 h ok (Gulch 44-45, Ridge 50-56, every buy at most 50% of the coins), smoke all ok (one run missed a tap at the top edge of a turned landscape view, the known rotate flake; the run after passed), perf Gulch 20.6 ms (6.9 ms script, 96 draw calls), empire 21.8 ms (7.5 ms script, 109 draw calls), falls-empire 24.3 ms (7.8 ms script, 104 draw calls, 65k triangles), update and install ok. Not yet tried on a real phone.

  - Notes: shipped 2026.11.19-2. **Prices** (`fmtPrice`, `sellsFor` in `src/ui/format.js`): one decimal under 10 coins ("0.9 coins"), "under 0.1 coin" below 0.05, never "0 coins"; the price breakdown's total reads the same way (`mulShown` now compares the product as the total is written) and a factor that rounds to 0 reads "× under 0.01" (was "× 0"). **Goods**: a good with every unit kept for towers reads "all kept for towers" with no price; Sell Off reads "worth 1.5 coins each" (the screenshot review read "sells for" next to Sell Off as a contradiction). **Rates**: every rate is written like the top bar (`fmtLive`, rounding down), and the Income sheet shows the very numbers the top bar last wrote (it took its own sample and read +28/s beside the bar's +25/s). **Coin costs** on tower and path cards read "9.99M / 2.31K coins", red when short, kept up with the purse 5 times a second in place (`paintCoinCosts`), so the card is not rebuilt for it. Building buttons keep "Upgrade · 120 coins". **Map**: the town card's rate reads "Earns here" (the top bar is every town together); a won outpost out of play reads "Sends goods" on its label and "Goods · Sent to Gulch" on its card (it sends its goods to the town in play, so it earned +0/s); an outpost waiting for a town reads "Needs Iron Ridge" (was "Start Iron Ridge"); a town ready to start has no "Ready to start" beside its Start button. **Goals**: a goal that sends the player to another town (`sendsAway` in `src/core/goals.js`: `there` or `away`) stays off the strip during a boss wave in the town in play; the bots read goals as before. **Words**: a boss always "got through" (was also "broke into" and "reached your vault"), raiders "got through to the stockpile" (was "broke into"), a vault "falls"; Heavy Cannon "Bigger blast, reaches farther, fires slower." and Siege Mortar "Shoots farther with a bigger blast, but fires slower." (both already fired at 0.85 times the rate, so no balance change); the back card names the button as it reads ("Tap ◂ Dry Creek next to Map to go back to the town you played last.", an explainer's new optional `say(h)`). Tests: `test/review19.test.js` (5). Smoke: a tower card's coins read have / need, red when short and plain when not. Checks: 313 tests, audit ok, balance ok and unchanged (no simulation change: the charter offered at 37.7-43.2 min, every strategy within 25%, variety ok), firstplay ok (newcomer Dry Creek 12.5 min, Gulch wave 10 10.3 min), idle ok (Capital 3.17-3.46 h, every buy at most 50% of the coins), smoke all ok (2 of 4 runs missed a tap at the top edge of a zoomed landscape town view, the known rotate flake, in code this box did not touch; the published build passed once in between), perf Gulch 20.6 ms (6.8 ms script, 96 draw calls), empire 21.5 ms (7.1 ms script, 109 draw calls), falls-empire 23.8 ms (7.8 ms script, 104 draw calls, 65k triangles), update and install ok. **Screenshot-only review**, left for later: the lost toast on the map says "Tap Map" while the map is open, and covers a label (the toasts item in the box below); the lost card repeats the goal strip's "Build more towers, then tap Resume waves"; "Vault 100%"; the locked labels "Wave 30 in Iron Ridge" should say they open something; the town labels' rates do not add up to the top bar's (each is smoothed on its own); in landscape the goods strip runs under a side card (the landscape item below); "kept for this path, not sold" explains the game. Not yet tried on a real phone.
- [x] **Landscape cards, toasts and Pine Hollow (split from Pacing and Evaluation 2, the rest).** In landscape a side card covered the coins and cut Pause and Hold waves, some cards pushed Upgrade below the edge, toasts sat over Hold waves and over sheets, the newcomer never played Pine Hollow, and the review of 2026.11.19-2 left three lines.
  - Notes: shipped 2026.11.19-3. **Landscape**: the play screen measures the bottom of the coins and side buttons and of the Pause row 5 times a second and sets `--cardtop` (only when it changes); on short landscape screens cards and sheets end there at the top, so they never cover Pause, Hold waves or the coins (the Automation sheet too). **Buttons in view**: a building card's Build or Upgrade row, and a tower card's cost, short line and Upgrade row (`.buybar`), stick to the card's bottom edge while it scrolls, at every size. Decision: a tower's path choice is too tall to pin on a 400 px screen (it covered the card's name), so at the path level the choice comes first, right under the name, and the stats follow. **Toasts**: each toast is placed again 350 ms after it shows and when a town's top bar mounts (`placeToasts`), so "Iron Ridge is open." lands below the new town's bar, not on Pause; sheets count as cards for `clearOfCards`, so a toast moves beside a side sheet. **Pine Hollow**: once the Blast Furnace stands the Ridge's strip says "Tap Map, Pine Hollow, then Play Pine Hollow" until Pine Hollow is won; Pine Hollow's strip has a Crossbow, the Clay Bank, 3 Crossbows, "Beat wave 15 to win Pine Hollow", then "Tap Map, Iron Ridge, then Play Iron Ridge" (`r-pine`, `p-*` in `src/data/goals.js`, `wonIn` and `inPlay` on the goal context). The careful bots already won it first, so their numbers did not move (r-pine done 75-82 min). New newcomer gate (`PINE_WON`): when the run leaves 55 min after the Ridge, it must go to Pine Hollow and win it within 40 min of getting there. **Review lines**: the lost toast on the map reads "Tap Copper Gulch, then Play Copper Gulch, then Resume waves." (no Tap Map); the strip hides while the lost card is up, so the two never say the same thing on one screen, and comes back as "Build more towers, then tap Resume waves" after Got it; locked map badges read "Opens at wave 52 in Iron Ridge". **Bug found on the way**: when a finished goal showed Done and no goal came next, the strip stayed up with the old goal's words; it now hides. Tests: `test/pinegoals.test.js` (2), `test/loco.test.js` walks past r-pine. Smoke (`uncovered`): a tower card with its damage open, a building card with its output open, and the Automation sheet never cover Pause, Hold waves or the coins, never run off the screen, and keep Build or Upgrade inside the card, at phone, landscape and desktop. - [x] **Words from the review of 2026.11.19-3 (split from Pacing and Evaluation 2, the rest).** What the screenshot-only review flagged and this box left:
  - The lost toast still shows over the map's town labels for a moment when you open the map right after a loss.

Decision: the small labels over the map keep "+26/s", the same as the top bar. The Silver Falls badge reads "Needs 5 deeds" (was "5 deeds", which read as if you had them). Auto-sell, routes and the Capital's contract also leave a floor of 120 s of the town's own output, capped at 2 times the towers' need (uncapped, it held back 14,400 timber in a test and stopped early sales). **Decision: workshops leave the towers' need alone too** (`WORKSHOP.spare` in `src/core/economy.js`, reading `town.keepUse`, the reserve without the floor). The bots showed auto-sell was not what made a tower buy wait: the Smelter ate the ore a Cannon needed and the Sawmill the timber of a Crossbow upgrade. A workshop held back that way is marked `starved = 'towers'`: its card says "Your towers get the timber first. This runs again when there is more.", its breakdown row reads "Timber kept for towers", and it is not a red problem tag, a map alert or the starved explainer. **Keep for towers · Normal / Lots** on the Goods sheet (the box said "Stock for towers"; the screenshot review flagged three names for one idea, so it took the building cards' word) (`setStock`, town.stock, save v19, 'stock' explainer), Lots keeps 2 times as much. The Goods sheet already said "N kept for towers" per good. **The measure** (`tools/reserve.js`): a wait counts only when the bots' best buy has its coins, lacks a good, and the yard held enough of that good since the town's last tower buy (it went to the market or a workshop). A Mine just built, or a Mortar before any Kiln, is waiting on a maker, which no reserve fixes. The new **saver** player plays like the owner: the active bot holding its coins 4 minutes in every 10 (waves held), then buying up to 8 in a burst. Old rule against new, 3 seeds: saver 16 against 1 burst buys missed (cut 94%, the gate), Active 0 and 0, Newcomer 0 and 0, Idle 0 and 0 (they spend as soon as they can, so they never meet the case; under the first, looser measure they were Active 292 against 228 s and Newcomer 1,160 against 1,028 s, nearly all a Mortar before a Kiln or a new Mine filling up). **Bot fix**: the careful player now builds the tower kind the goal strip asks for once it can pay, even with all the towers it wants (seed 4 had a Mortar's coins and goods from minute 25 and built none until minute 61, failing g-mortar). Not swarm, which raises every tower evenly (a lone level-1 Mortar held its wave 20 back to 21.2 min). Smoke's goods-strip tap now stocks more timber than the towers keep, so the price rows show. Eight old tests asserted the old reserve or ran workshops with it, updated, plus new tests (workshops spare the reserve, Stock for towers and its migration). The best two-stretch spots (between the passes, inside the bends) were already pads, so the new ones stand along the outer sides and corners. **Extra towers cost more**: a town's normal towers past `board.crowd` (its old pad count) cost 25% more coin and goods each (`EXTRA_TOWER`, `crowdFactor` in `src/core/defense.js`); upgrades and the Mortar cost the same. The stone pad card says "You have 12 towers here. Each new one costs more than the last." and a one-time explainer (`crowd`) says it once. **What the bots found and the fixes**: (1) a fourth Falls pit on the east side gave the Mortar 39-45% of the damage, so it was dropped. (2) Any Falls pad near where a lane comes out of its entrance (the river's mouth, then the rock cut at [7.9, -.75] and [7.6, -1.6]) gave the Frost Lens 40-46% of the damage for active, economy and defense, since raiders leave the entrance bunched and its pulse hits them all; those pads moved to the east side ([11.4, 4]). (3) With the Frost Lens back to 21-28%, the economy player's one Cannon did 37.4% in the Falls (35.4% on the old pads, already near the 36% cap), so the Cannon now grows 1.24 a level like the Frost Lens (was 1.25; `test/growth.test.js` allows equal). (4) Pine Hollow: min-max sat at 3 towers there (two Crossbows and one Cannon, its per-kind caps meant for five kinds) while every other strategy grew onto the new pads, 11.8 min against 8.4. Decision: in an outpost with two tower kinds the min-max bot may build half its tower count of each (`capOf` in `tools/balance.js`), since a real few-strong-towers player there would still build 4-5; it is a bot fix, not a target change. (5) Idle seed 6 (hours 7-12 17.07M against 18.70M) passes after (3): 20.82M against 17.27M. **A lasting check**: `test/pads.test.js` fails a new pad within 2.5 of the first 3 units of a lane past its entrance in a town with the Frost Lens (the Gulch's pads 16 and 17 are listed, measured fine). `tools/shots.js` adds crowd. Some edge pads (Iron Ridge's west and south ones) show partly cut at the home view on a phone, as some older ones did; smoke taps every one, and the look pass can widen the frames.
